</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A8_zH65e9NLPlz" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company has four distinct lines of business; Contract Manufacturing, Custom and White Label, Consumer Branded Products, and Medical
Devices/Other.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Contract
Manufacturing</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Customers
order rolls of gel (&#8220;rollstock&#8221;). The rollstock is shipped to our customers, which they package into finished goods.
Historically, this has been the Company&#8217;s primary source of revenue.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Custom
and White Label</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">These
products often infuse various ingredients into our base gel to develop unique product offerings to satisfy market demand (e.g. aloe infused
into the gel for a beauty mask). The rollstock is converted and packaged into salable units. The finished goods are shipped to the customer,
who is ultimately responsible for product distribution. Frequently these products started as development deals, in which the customer
paid the Company a small fee to develop a specific product. Once completed, the customer places an order for newly developed product.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Consumer
Branded Products</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">These
products are finished goods marketed and sold directly to consumers by the Company through online and retail channels. We are responsible
for sales, marketing, and distribution. The products we sell under our MedaGel brand primarily relate to healthcare over-the-counter
(&#8220;OTC&#8221;) remedy solutions, such as blister and applications. In December 2023 we added a second consumer product brand when
we completed the purchase of the Kenkoderm brand. The Kenkoderm skincare line was originally developed by a dermatologist to provide
gentle to the skin products for consumer with psoriasis. In May 2024, we added our third consumer product brand with the purchase of
the Silly George brand. Silly George is a beauty brand primarily focused on false eyelashes and other eye related products. We continue
to look for additional potential acquisitions as part of our consumer product &#8220;roll-up&#8221; strategy.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Medical
Devices/Other</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Medical
Devices are a hybrid business, combining elements of Custom &amp; White Label and Consumer Branded Products. Medical Devices, which are
not yet marketed, are expected to be distributed through strategic partnerships. We will manufacture and possibly convert/package the
device while the strategic partner brings the product to market. Small market Medical Devices could be launched by us, but also be offered
to a distributor to reach the full scale of the market.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Other
includes freight charged to customers who purchase the Company&#8217;s branded consumer products through their Shopify stores.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 13; Value: 2 -->
    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->13<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>
    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="width: 100%"><a href="#toc_001">Table of Contents</a></td></tr></table></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Shipping
and Handling Revenue and Expense</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Shipping
and handling revenue and expense are included in our condensed consolidated statements of operations in revenues and cost of revenues,
respectively. The Company accounts for shipping activities, consisting of direct costs to ship products performed after the risk of loss
passes to the customer. Shipping revenue and expense are primarily generated through the Amazon marketplace and Silly George direct customer
sales.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000609" name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationOptionAndIncentivePlansPolicy"><p id="xdx_843_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationOptionAndIncentivePlansPolicy_zNHEwhWDq0A2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Share-based
Compensation</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
August 28, 2019, the Company adopted the 2019 Long-Term Incentive Plan, as amended (the &#8220;2019 Plan&#8221;). See Note 15 for further
details regarding the 2019 Plan.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
2019 Plan provides certain employees, contractors, and outside directors with share-based compensation in the form of incentive stock
options, non-qualified stock options, stock appreciation rights, restricted stock, restricted stock units, performance awards, dividend
equivalent rights and other awards. The fair values of incentive stock option award grants are estimated as of the date of grant using
a Black-Scholes option valuation model. Compensation expense is recognized in the consolidated statements of operations on a straight-line
basis over the requisite service period, which is generally the vesting period.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000611" name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_840_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock_zyzp3EEmIIG7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Income
Taxes</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Income
taxes are accounted for using an asset and liability approach that requires the recognition of deferred tax assets and liabilities for
the expected future tax consequences of temporary differences between the financial statement and tax bases of assets and liabilities
at the applicable tax rates. Deferred tax assets are reduced by a valuation allowance when it is more likely than not that some portion
or all of the deferred tax assets will not be realized. Deferred tax assets and liabilities are adjusted for the effects of changes in
tax laws and rates.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Tax
benefits are recognized from an uncertain tax position only if it is more likely than not that the tax position will be sustained upon
examination by a tax authority and based upon the technical merits of the tax position. The tax benefit recognized in the consolidated
financial statements for a particular tax position is based on the largest benefit that is more likely than not to be realized upon settlement.
An unrecognized tax benefit, or a portion thereof, is presented in the consolidated financial statements as a reduction to a deferred
tax asset for a net operating loss carryforward, a similar tax loss, or a tax credit carryforward if such settlement is required or expected
in the event the uncertain tax position is disallowed.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000613" name="us-gaap:LesseeLeasesPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84C_eus-gaap--LesseeLeasesPolicyTextBlock_z6vhR9o5SNt8" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Leases</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">We
account for our leases in accordance with ASC 842, <i>Leases.</i> We determine whether an arrangement is an operating or financing lease
at contract inception. Operating leases, requires recognition of leases on the consolidated balance sheets as right-of-use (&#8220;ROU&#8221;)
assets and lease liabilities. ROU assets represent the Company&#8217;s right to use underlying assets for the lease terms and lease liabilities
represent the Company&#8217;s obligation to make lease payments arising from the leases. Operating lease ROU assets and operating lease
liabilities are recognized based on the present value and future minimum lease payments over the lease term at commencement date. As
the Company&#8217;s leases do not provide an implicit rate, the Company used its estimated incremental borrowing rate based on the information
available at commencement date in determining the present value of lease payments. A number of the lease agreements contain options to
renew and options to terminate the leases early. The lease term used to calculate ROU assets and lease liabilities only includes renewal
and termination options that are deemed reasonably certain to be exercised.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company recognized lease liabilities, with corresponding ROU assets, based on the present value of unpaid lease payments for existing
operating leases longer than twelve months. The ROU assets were adjusted per ASC 842 transition guidance for existing lease-related balances
of accrued and prepaid rent, and unamortized lease incentives provided by lessors. Operating lease cost is recognized as a single lease
cost on a straight-line basis over the lease term and is recorded in cost of revenues and selling, general and administrative expenses.
Variable lease payments for common area maintenance, property taxes and other operating expenses are recognized as expense in the year
when the changes in facts and circumstances on which the variable lease payments are based occur. The Company has elected not to separate
lease and non-lease components for all property leases for the purposes of calculating ROU assets and lease liabilities.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 14; Value: 2 -->
    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->14<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>
    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="width: 100%"><a href="#toc_001">Table of Contents</a></td></tr></table></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Financing
leases are those that transfer substantially all of the risks and rewards of ownership to the Company. At the lease commencement date,
the Company recognizes a financing lease ROU asset and a corresponding lease liability measured at the present value of future lease
payments. The ROU asset is subsequently amortized on a straight-line basis over the shorter of the lease term or the useful life of the
underlying asset, while the lease liability is increased by interest expense and reduced by lease payments made. Interest expense on
the lease liability and amortization of the ROU asset are presented separately in the consolidated statements of operations, resulting
in a front-loaded expense pattern over the lease term. Financing lease ROU assets are included in property and equipment, net, and the
related lease liabilities are included within current and long-term debt, as applicable.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000615" name="NXGL:VariableInterestEntityPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84F_ecustom--VariableInterestEntityPolicyTextBlock_zL2bc40C1Swd" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Variable
Interest Entity</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company reviews each legal entity formed by parties related to the Company to determine whether or not the Company has a variable interest
in the entity and whether or not the entity would meet the definition of a variable interest entity (&#8220;VIE&#8221;) in accordance
with ASC Topic 810, <i>Consolidation</i>. In assessing whether the Company has a variable interest in the entity as a whole, the Company
considers and makes judgements regarding the purpose and design of the entity, the value of the licensed assets to the entity, the value
of the entity&#8217;s total assets and the significant activities of the entity. If the Company has a variable interest in the entity
as a whole, the Company assesses whether or not the Company is a primary beneficiary of that VIE, based on a number of factors, including:
(i) which party has the power to direct the activities that most significantly affect the VIE&#8217;s economic performance, (ii) the
parties&#8217; contractual rights and responsibilities pursuant to the collaboration agreement, and (iii) which party has the obligation
to absorb losses of or the right to receive benefits from the VIE that could be significant to the VIE.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">If
the Company determines that it is the primary beneficiary of a VIE at the onset of the collaboration, the collaboration is treated as
a business combination and the Company consolidates the financial statements of the VIE into the Company&#8217;s consolidated financial
statements. On a quarterly basis, the Company will evaluate whether it continues to be the primary beneficiary of the consolidated VIE.
If the Company determines that it is no longer the primary beneficiary of a consolidated VIE, it deconsolidates the VIE in the period
in which the determination is made.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Assets
and liabilities recorded as a result of consolidating the financial results of the VIE into the Company&#8217;s consolidated balance
sheet do not represent additional assets that could be used to satisfy claims against the Company&#8217;s general assets or liabilities
for which creditors have recourse to the Company&#8217;s general assets.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000617" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84B_eus-gaap--NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zaZ1VKCXdev3" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Accounting
Pronouncements Issued But Not Yet Adopted </i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 18.55pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
December 2025<i>, </i>the FASB issued ASU <i>2025</i>-<i>12, Codification Improvements</i> to make improvements to the Codification arising
from technical corrections, unintended application of the Codification, and clarifications. For all entities, the amendments in this
Update are effective for annual reporting periods beginning after <i>December 15, 2026, </i>including interim periods within those annual
reporting periods. Early adoption is permitted. The Company is currently evaluating the impact this new guidance will have on its financial
statements and disclosures.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
November 2024, the FASB issued the ASC 2024-03, <i>Income Statement - Reporting Comprehensive Income - Expense Disaggregation Disclosures
(Subtopic 220-04) Disaggregation of Income Statement Expenses,</i> which requires additional disclosure of the nature of expenses included
in the income statement in response to requests from investors for more information about an entity&#8217;s expenses. The new standard
requires disclosures about specific types of expenses included in the expense captions presented on the face of the income statement
as disclosures about selling expenses. The guidance is effective for annual reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2026 and interim
reporting periods within annual reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2027. The requirements will be applied prospectively with
the option for retrospective application. Early adoption is permitted. The Company is currently evaluating the impact this new guidance
will have on its financial statements and disclosures.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_857_z9sXO5c8ZFTa" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 4.5pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 4.5pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 4.5pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000619" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80E_eus-gaap--SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock_zGGHlW6g10cg" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>4.
<span id="xdx_821_ztl9vE2xka1d">Business Segments</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company&#8217;s CODM evaluates the financial performance of the Company&#8217;s segments based upon segment operating income or (loss)
as the profitability measure. The Company has identified its Chief Executive Officer as the CODM. Items outside of operating income or
(loss) are not reported by segment, since they are excluded from the single measure of segment profitability reviewed by the CODM.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000621" name="us-gaap:ReconciliationOfRevenueFromSegmentsToConsolidatedTextBlock"><p id="xdx_891_eus-gaap--ReconciliationOfRevenueFromSegmentsToConsolidatedTextBlock_zDuA2ng1mP08" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Summarized

below.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span><span id="xdx_8B4_zF7R6c1q4v1e" style="display: none">Schedule of Reportable Segments</span></span><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000695" name="us-gaap:VariableInterestEntityDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_807_eus-gaap--VariableInterestEntityDisclosureTextBlock_zZjWamdnpgti" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>5.
<span id="xdx_825_zHOKcnz1oSg6">Variable Interest Entities</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Interest
in Joint Venture &#8211; CGN</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
March 1, 2023, the Company acquired a <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionPercentageOfVotingInterestsAcquired_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20230301__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--CGConvertingAndPackagingLLCMember_zivWYMTFubq1" title="Acquisition percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessAcquisitionPercentageOfVotingInterestsAcquired" contextRef="AsOf2023-03-01_custom_CGConvertingAndPackagingLLCMember" id="Fact000697" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">50</ix:nonFraction></span>% interest in the CGN JV (see Note 1). The CGN JV is owned <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionPercentageOfVotingInterestsAcquired_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20230301__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--CGConvertingAndPackagingLLCMember_zFD17lhLQ421" title="Acquisition percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessAcquisitionPercentageOfVotingInterestsAcquired" contextRef="AsOf2023-03-01_custom_CGConvertingAndPackagingLLCMember" id="Fact000699" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">50</ix:nonFraction></span>% by the Company and <span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionPercentageOfVotingInterestsAcquired_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20230301__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--CGLaboratoriesIncMember_z6FvJpqrAnJ4" title="Acquisition percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessAcquisitionPercentageOfVotingInterestsAcquired" contextRef="AsOf2023-03-01_custom_CGLaboratoriesIncMember" id="Fact000701" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">50</ix:nonFraction></span>% by CG Labs.
CG Labs contributed its existing converting and packaging division to the CGN JV, including, but not limited to, its facilities, equipment,
employees, and customers. The Company will contribute $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireBusinessesNetOfCashAcquired_pn3n3_c20230301__20230301__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--CGConvertingAndPackagingLLCMember_zsrSGaKd8Z5h" title="Payment to acquire business"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireBusinessesNetOfCashAcquired" contextRef="From2023-03-012023-03-01_custom_CGConvertingAndPackagingLLCMember" id="Fact000703" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">500</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand to the CGN JV, on a schedule to be determined, to be used for equipment
and facility upgrades as well as general corporate purposes for the CGN JV.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
CGN JV is considered to be a VIE and we have consolidated the CGN JV, because we believe we are the primary beneficiary and we meet the
power and the economics criteria.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
following table presents the assets and liabilities of the CGN JV, included in the condensed consolidated balance sheet as of March 31,
2026 and December 31, 2025. The assets and liabilities presented below include only the third-party assets and liabilities of the consolidated
VIE and excludes any intercompany balances, which were eliminated upon consolidation. .</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000782" name="us-gaap:LesseeOperatingLeasesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_805_eus-gaap--LesseeOperatingLeasesTextBlock_z1UXt8NZgKvi" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>6.
<span id="xdx_82F_z6bGTDduaJD4">Operating Leases</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company has an operating lease for a commercial manufacturing facility and administrative offices located in Langhorne, Pennsylvania
that runs through January 2031. <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--LesseeOperatingLeaseOptionToExtend_c20260101__20260331__srt--StatementGeographicalAxis__country--PA_zJl1Gne0QxV1" title="Option to extend"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_country_PA" id="Fact000784" name="us-gaap:LesseeOperatingLeaseOptionToExtend">There are two options that can extend the lease term for five years each</ix:nonNumeric></span>. The exercise of the lease options
to renew is solely at the Company&#8217;s discretion.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company also has a sublease for office and manufacturing space in Granbury, Texas that runs through February 2028. The Company modified
the lease agreement through July 2035.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000823" name="us-gaap:LesseeFinanceLeasesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_eus-gaap--LesseeFinanceLeasesTextBlock_zFELnieS997" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>7.
<span id="xdx_824_zYEeGqNZUmw7">Financing Lease</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
February 2024, the CGN JV entered into a lease agreement for certain equipment under separate non-cancelable equipment loan and security
agreements. The agreement matures in January 2030. The agreements require monthly payments of principal and interest through maturity
and are secured by the assets under the lease. As of March 31, 2026, $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--FinanceLeaseRightOfUseAsset_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--FairValueByAssetClassAxis__us-gaap--PropertyPlantAndEquipmentMember_zZK9DEhL6Wgc" title="Financing lease liability included in property and equipment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:FinanceLeaseRightOfUseAsset" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_PropertyPlantAndEquipmentMember" id="Fact000825" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">419</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand is included in the property and equipment on the balance
sheet. The weighted average interest rate was <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageDiscountRatePercent_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331_zrGuifR9i4N5" title="Weighted average interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageDiscountRatePercent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000827" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">9.1</ix:nonFraction></span>% and <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageDiscountRatePercent_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20251231_zgRblNpiXmY2" title="Weighted average interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageDiscountRatePercent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000829" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">9.1</ix:nonFraction></span>% and the weighted average remaining lease term was <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageRemainingLeaseTerm1_iI_dtY_c20260331_zkAPLJh7MFjk" title="Weighted average remaining lease term"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" format="ixt-sec:duryear" id="Fact000831" name="us-gaap:FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageRemainingLeaseTerm1">3.8</ix:nonNumeric></span> years and <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageRemainingLeaseTerm1_iI_dtY_c20251231_ziYfqMHn2OW9" title="Weighted average remaining lease term"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" format="ixt-sec:duryear" id="Fact000833" name="us-gaap:FinanceLeaseWeightedAverageRemainingLeaseTerm1">4.8</ix:nonNumeric></span> years
at March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A6_zPiFu7ISgzB6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
connection with the May 15, 2024 acquisition of Silly George, the Company identified intangible assets of $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIntangibles_iI_pn3n3_c20240515__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--SillyGeorgeAcquisitionMember__us-gaap--FiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsByMajorClassAxis__custom--TechnologyRelatedAndCustomerRelatedIntangiblesMember_zns9xjtdb2Oh" title="Intangible assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIntangibles" contextRef="AsOf2024-05-15_custom_SillyGeorgeAcquisitionMember_custom_TechnologyRelatedAndCustomerRelatedIntangiblesMember" id="Fact000955" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">600</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand representing
trademark related intangibles with indefinite lives.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Intangible
assets with indefinite lives are tested for impairment within one year of the acquisition date or annually as of December 31, and whenever
indicators of impairment exist.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
intangible assets with definite lives are being amortized on a straight-line basis over their weighted average estimated useful life
of <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--AcquiredFiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsWeightedAverageUsefulLife_dtY_c20260101__20260331_zPEAHC8D0N62" title="Expected life"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" format="ixt-sec:duryear" id="Fact000957" name="us-gaap:AcquiredFiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsWeightedAverageUsefulLife">1.5</ix:nonNumeric></span> years and amortization expense amounted to $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets_pn3n3_c20260101__20260331_zddK9UzReAq3" title="Amortization expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000959" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">26</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets_pn3n3_c20250101__20250331_zpwLzalmJT13" title="Amortization expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000961" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">32</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000994" name="us-gaap:DebtDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80E_eus-gaap--DebtDisclosureTextBlock_zXoRxxVJKLx4" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>12.
<span id="xdx_821_zvzHu6XTObEg">Notes Payable</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>CGN
Segment</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
CGN JV has entered into two separate promissory note agreements for the purchase of equipment. These notes have a term of <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentTerm_dc_c20240313__20240313__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--JVNotesPayableMember_zdUnwyiPR7G9" title="Agreements terms"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-03-132024-03-13_custom_JVNotesPayableMember" format="ixt-sec:durwordsen" id="Fact000996" name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentTerm">five years</ix:nonNumeric></span>
beginning on March 13, 2024, accrue interest at <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20240313__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--JVNotesPayableMember_zTQxlKOSgop2" title="Interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-13_custom_JVNotesPayableMember" id="Fact000998" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">8</ix:nonFraction></span>% and require monthly payments of $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentPeriodicPayment_pn3n3_c20240313__20240313__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--JVNotesPayableMember_zL2uBxyceXDk" title="Debt instrument periodic payment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentPeriodicPayment" contextRef="From2024-03-132024-03-13_custom_JVNotesPayableMember" id="Fact001000" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">9</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand, collectively, until maturity. The total
principal balance outstanding was $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--JVNotesPayableMember_zHfEZL4lpqbg" title="Principal balance amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_JVNotesPayableMember" id="Fact001002" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">297</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand and $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_pn3n3_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--JVNotesPayableMember_z1oiV3FZvF85" title="Principal balance amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_JVNotesPayableMember" id="Fact001004" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">321</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>NexGel
Segment</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company has entered into a $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--NotesPayable_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--NexGelMember_zESivvNgJP7a" title="Notes payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_NexGelMember28695609" id="Fact001006" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">13</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand promissory note agreement for certain leasehold improvements. The Outstanding principal amount
of $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_pn3n3_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--NexGelMember_zmPBaIBubQ14" title="Principal balance amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_NexGelMember28695609" id="Fact001008" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand as of December 31, 2025 was repaid in full in January of 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Economic
Injury Disaster Loan</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
May 28, 2020, the Company entered into the standard loan documents required for securing a loan (the &#8220;EIDL Loan&#8221;) from the
SBA under its Economic Injury Disaster Loan (&#8220;EIDL&#8221;) assistance program in light of the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on
the Company&#8217;s business. Pursuant to that certain Loan Authorization and Agreement (the &#8220;SBA Loan Agreement&#8221;), the principal
amount of the EIDL Loan is up to $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityMaximumBorrowingCapacity_iI_pn3n3_c20200528__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember_zzkc3WoI1M52" title="Borrowing capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityMaximumBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2020-05-28_custom_EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember" id="Fact001010" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">260,500</ix:nonFraction></span>, with proceeds to be used for working capital purposes. Interest accrues at the rate of <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20200528__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember_zz5IpvCLRkX4" title="Interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2020-05-28_custom_EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember" id="Fact001012" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">3.75</ix:nonFraction></span>%
per annum. Installment payments, including principal and interest, are due monthly beginning May 28, 2021 (twelve months from the date
of the SBA Note) in the amount of $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentPeriodicPayment_pn3n3_c20200527__20200528__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember_zhSdTaN0BWrj" title="Debt instrument periodic payment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentPeriodicPayment" contextRef="From2020-05-272020-05-28_custom_EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember" id="Fact001014" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,270</ix:nonFraction></span>. The balance of principal and interest is payable thirty years from the date of the SBA Note.
In connection therewith, the Company received an $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceOfDebt_pn3n3_c20200527__20200528__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember_ze9ECORxUDbj" title="Loan advance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfDebt" contextRef="From2020-05-272020-05-28_custom_EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember" id="Fact001016" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">8</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand advance, which does not have to be repaid. On March 26, 2021, the SBA announced
that all EIDL loans issued in 2020 will start repayment 24 months from the date of the SBA Note. The SBA has since extended the repayment
start to 30 months from the date of the SBA Note. The Company made its first payment in December 2022. The balances of the principal
and accrued interest amounted to $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentIncreaseAccruedInterest_pn3n3_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember_zCp8oC9QoVga" title="Principal and accrued interest"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentIncreaseAccruedInterest" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember" id="Fact001018" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">265</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentIncreaseAccruedInterest_pn3n3_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember_zooce8IipExe" title="Principal and accrued interest"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentIncreaseAccruedInterest" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_custom_EconomicInjuryDisasterLoanMember" id="Fact001020" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">267</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact001042" name="us-gaap:DerivativesAndFairValueTextBlock"><p id="xdx_802_eus-gaap--DerivativesAndFairValueTextBlock_zHef9DDyjYA3" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>13.
<span id="xdx_82D_z7sCGQRlOO99">Convertible Notes Payable</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
February 10, 2026, the Company issued $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceOfSecuredDebt_pn3n3_c20260210__20260210__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember_zACYq2o7HGXd" title="Cash proceeds"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfSecuredDebt" contextRef="From2026-02-102026-02-10_custom_SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact001044" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,797</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand aggregate principal amount of Series A Senior Secured Convertible Note (the &#8220;Series
A Convertible Note&#8221;) for cash proceeds of $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromConvertibleDebt_pn3n3_c20260210__20260210__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember_za9G0QFZAJzd" title="Cash proceeds"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromConvertibleDebt" contextRef="From2026-02-102026-02-10_custom_SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact001046" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,618</ix:nonFraction> </span>thousand. The Series A Convertible Note matures on February 10, 2028, which is
two years from issuance, unless earlier converted, redeemed or repurchased, and bears interest at <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260210_zfCz4Hh7fHW3" title="Debt interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-02-10" id="Fact001048" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">10</ix:nonFraction></span>% per annum. Interest is payable
in arrears on the first trading date of each calendar month beginning May 1, 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
holder may convert the Series A Convertible Note or any portion of the Series A Convertible Note, at their option, into common shares
of the Company. The number of common shares to be issued will equal 110% of outstanding principal and accrued unpaid interest converted
at the conversion price of $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1_iI_pid_c20260210__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember_zUz20xFYD8H1" title="Debt conversion price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1" contextRef="AsOf2026-02-10_custom_SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact001050" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">1.244</ix:nonFraction></span> per share (the &#8220;initial conversion price&#8221;). The conversion price is subject to adjustment
if certain conditions are met.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Convertible Note was issued at a $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentUnamortizedDiscount_iI_pn3n3_c20260331_zBfemiRFK8w7" title="Debt discount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentUnamortizedDiscount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact001052" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">180</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand discount, representing the difference between the principal amount and cash proceeds received. This discount is being
amortized to interest expense over the contractual term of the Series A Convertible Note using the effective interest method. For
the three month period ended March 31, 2026, the Company recognized contractual coupon interest of $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--ContractualObligation_iI_pn3n3_c20260331_z9mfWSvc3My3" title="Debt contractual amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractualObligation" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact001054" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">23</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand and amortization of debt discount of $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--AmortizationOfDebtDiscountPremium_pn3n3_c20260101__20260331_zvsIVHyJdKk8" title="Amortization of debt discount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AmortizationOfDebtDiscountPremium" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact001056" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">11</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand, for total interest expense related to the Series A Convertible Note of $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--InterestExpense_pn3n3_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember_zaG7CYYKxTj6" title="Interest expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestExpense" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_SeriesAConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact001058" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">34</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand. The effective interest rate is approximately <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateEffectivePercentage_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331_zGGoLfZMm0Vi" title="Debt effective interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateEffectivePercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact001060" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">10.5</ix:nonFraction></span>%.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
March 12, 2026, the holder converted $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DebtConversionConvertedInstrumentAmount1_pn3n3_c20260312__20260312_zHLKr9TlRoQ8" title="Debt converted principal amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtConversionConvertedInstrumentAmount1" contextRef="From2026-03-122026-03-12" id="Fact001062" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">350,000</ix:nonFraction></span> of principal amount and unpaid interest of the note into <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--DebtConversionConvertedInstrumentSharesIssued1_c20260312__20260312_zYyHhhfgp4Nf" title="Debt converted principal amount, shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtConversionConvertedInstrumentSharesIssued1" contextRef="From2026-03-122026-03-12" id="Fact001064" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">312,235</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock
pursuant to the contractual conversion terms. The Company accounted for the transaction as a debt conversion and reclassified the carrying
amount of the converted portion of the Series A Convertible Note, including the related unamortized discount, to stockholders&#8217;
equity, and no gain or loss was recognized on the conversion.</span></p>

price of $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementsByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodWeightedAverageExercisePrice_c20260212__20260212__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--EmployeeStockOptionMember_zwmJGfSrt4Vk" title="Exercise price of options"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementsByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodWeightedAverageExercisePrice" contextRef="From2026-02-122026-02-12_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_us-gaap_EmployeeStockOptionMember" id="Fact001126" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">1.31</ix:nonFraction></span> to the former Chief Financial Officer pursuant to the terms of a separation agreement dated February 4, 2026, all of which

of March 31, 2026, vested outstanding stock options had $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsOutstandingIntrinsicValue_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--StockOptionMember_zormdW0GzF2l" title="Intrinsic value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsOutstandingIntrinsicValue" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_StockOptionMember" id="Fact001170" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">12</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand intrinsic value as the exercise price is greater than the estimated
fair value of the underlying common stock, respectively. As of March 31, 2026, there was approximately $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--EmployeeServiceShareBasedCompensationNonvestedAwardsTotalCompensationCostNotYetRecognizedStockOptions_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--StockOptionMember_z4scSZu7zNu4" title="Unrecognized share based compensation"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EmployeeServiceShareBasedCompensationNonvestedAwardsTotalCompensationCostNotYetRecognizedStockOptions" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_StockOptionMember" id="Fact001172" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">336</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand of total unrecognized

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company recognizes compensation expense for stock option awards on a straight-line basis over the applicable service period of the award.
The service period is generally the vesting period.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

following assumptions were used to calculate share-based compensation expense for three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025:</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A2_zsifloAMxNGa" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company does not have sufficient historical information to develop reasonable expectations about future exercise patterns and post-vesting
employment termination behavior. Accordingly, the Company has elected to use the &#8220;simplified method&#8221; to estimate the expected
term of its share-based awards. The simplified method computes the expected term as the sum of the award&#8217;s vesting term plus the
original contractual term divided by two.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 23; Value: 2 -->
    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->23<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>
    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="width: 100%"><a href="#toc_001">Table of Contents</a></td></tr></table></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company estimated the expected volatility input for the Black-Scholes model using the historical volatility of its own publicly traded
common stock over a period commensurate with the expected term of the option.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company recognizes compensation expense for stock option awards on a straight-line basis over the applicable service period of the award.
The service period is generally the vesting period.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Restrictive
stock awards</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Effective
February 12, 2026, the Company granted an aggregate of <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsGrantsInPeriod_pid_c20260212__20260212__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_zRXKwfjyN9Z" title="Granted, shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsGrantsInPeriod" contextRef="From2026-02-122026-02-12_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact001196" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">20,325</ix:nonFraction></span></span>
<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">fully vested shares of its common stock to its former Chief
Financial Officer pursuant to the terms of a separation agreement dated February 4, 2026. An additional <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsVestedInPeriod_pid_c20260204__20260204__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_zoXuOvMfmIEa" title="Shares authorized for issuance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsVestedInPeriod" contextRef="From2026-02-042026-02-04_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact001198" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">20,325</ix:nonFraction></span></span>

has measured the value of the <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsVestedInPeriod_pid_c20260212__20260212__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_zjtiFszylGGj"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsVestedInPeriod" contextRef="From2026-02-122026-02-12_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact001199" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">40,650</ix:nonFraction></span></span>

<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">per share).</span></p>

expense for forfeited awards. The Company recognizes the reversal of any previously recognized compensation expense on forfeited awards
in the period the awards are forfeited. As of March 31, 2026, there was $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--EmployeeServiceShareBasedCompensationNonvestedAwardsTotalCompensationCostNotYetRecognizedShareBasedAwardsOtherThanOptions_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_zudFOy6uTHGf" title="Unrecognized share based compensation expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EmployeeServiceShareBasedCompensationNonvestedAwardsTotalCompensationCostNotYetRecognizedShareBasedAwardsOtherThanOptions" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact001229" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">29</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand unrecognized share-based compensation related to

price is greater than the estimated fair value of the underlying common stock.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact001279" name="us-gaap:CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80D_eus-gaap--CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock_zJjVv99duJma" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>16.
<span id="xdx_829_zLIBhIsW3Lz1">Commitments and Contingencies</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Partnership
Advance</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
July 14, 2025, the Company expanded its partnership with STADA Arzneimittel AG (&#8220;STADA&#8221;), a European leader in consumer health.
The expansion included a $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromPartnershipContribution_pn6n6_c20250714__20250714_znIzQMvB5yne" title="Partnership accrued advance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromPartnershipContribution" contextRef="From2025-07-142025-07-14" id="Fact001281" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1</ix:nonFraction></span> million advance from STADA to the Company in non-dilutive capital to support product launches and marketing
efforts under the Master Distribution Agreement between the parties and relates to the planned launch of digestive enzyme formulas and
solutions targeting scars and stretch marks. As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the Company held $<span id="xdx_90E_ecustom--AdvanceInPartnershipRestrictedCash_iI_pn3n3_c20260331_zfG54bCZaDe9" title="Advance in partnership restricted cash"><ix:nonFraction name="NXGL:AdvanceInPartnershipRestrictedCash" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact001283" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">612</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand and $<span id="xdx_905_ecustom--AdvanceInPartnershipRestrictedCash_iI_pn3n3_c20251231_zPCUY7o2r2Lb" title="Advance in partnership restricted cash"><ix:nonFraction name="NXGL:AdvanceInPartnershipRestrictedCash" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact001285" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">741</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand,
respectively, of restricted cash related to advances received under partnership arrangements. Correspondingly, $<span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--PartnershipRestrictedCashAdvance_iI_pn3n3_c20260331_zMjgvQmlu9z" title="Partnership accrued advance"><ix:nonFraction name="NXGL:PartnershipRestrictedCashAdvance" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact001287" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">596</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand and $<span id="xdx_903_ecustom--PartnershipRestrictedCashAdvance_iI_pn3n3_c20251231_z1h7kyGWpInc" title="Partnership accrued advance"><ix:nonFraction name="NXGL:PartnershipRestrictedCashAdvance" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact001289" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">731</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand, respectively, were recorded as current liabilities within partnership accrued advances. The advance is subject to contractual
restrictions on use and will be applied against eligible project costs as incurred in accordance with the terms of the Master Distribution
Agreement, as amended.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0pt; text-align: justify"><i>License agreement </i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0pt; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0pt; text-align: justify">Under the License Agreement described in Note 1, the Company may be required to make contingent milestone payments
of up to $<span id="xdx_909_ecustom--MaximumContingentMilestonePayment_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331_zHPC8VzMUSDh" title="Maximum contingent milestone payments"><ix:nonFraction name="NXGL:MaximumContingentMilestonePayment" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact001291" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">17.5</ix:nonFraction></span> million to Celularity upon the achievement of specified commercial milestones.</p>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Litigation
</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company may be subject to legal proceedings and claims that arise in the ordinary course of business. Management is not currently aware
of any matters that will or may have a material effect on the financial position, results of operations, or cash flows of the Company.</span></p>

balance exceeding such limit. The Company has not experienced any credit losses associated with its cash balances in the past. The Company
invests its cash equivalents in U.S. treasury bills with original maturities of three months or less.</span></p>

securities are comprised of U.S. treasury bills with original maturities greater than three months. The Company has not experienced any
losses in such accounts. The Company believes it is not exposed to any significant credit risk on cash, cash equivalents, and marketable
securities and performs periodic evaluations of the credit standing of such institutions.</span></p>
